const nextConfig: NextConfig = {
  agentRules: false,
  distDir: process.env.NEXT_DIST_DIR ?? ".next",
  output: "standalone",
  outputFileTracingRoot: fileURLToPath(new URL("../..", import.meta.url)),
  reactCompiler: true,
  transpilePackages: ["@my-app/ui"],
  typedRoutes: true,
};

export default nextConfig;

#   next-self-orpc-sqlite-better-auth-docker
#   next-self-orpc-sqlite-better-auth-docker-e2e
#   next-self-orpc-sqlite-better-auth-expo-docker
#   next-self-orpc-sqlite-better-auth-expo-docker-e2e
import { HydrationBoundary, dehydrate } from "@tanstack/react-query";
import { redirect } from "next/navigation";

import { TodoList } from "#src/components/todo-list.tsx";
import { getQueryClient, serverApi } from "#src/server/query.ts";
import { getSession } from "#src/server/session.ts";

const TodosPage = async () => {
  if (!(await getSession())) {
    redirect("/login?redirect=%2Ftodos");
  }

  const queryClient = getQueryClient();
  await queryClient.query(serverApi.todos.list.queryOptions());

  return (
    <HydrationBoundary state={dehydrate(queryClient)}>
      <TodoList />
    </HydrationBoundary>
  );
};

export default TodosPage;

export const Header = async () => {
  const session = await getSession();

  return (
    <header className="bg-background/80 sticky top-0 z-10 border-b backdrop-blur-lg">
      <div className="mx-auto flex h-14 max-w-5xl items-center gap-3 px-5 sm:gap-6 sm:px-8">

          <UserMenu user={session?.user ?? null} />
          <ModeToggle />
        </div>
      </div>
    </header>
  );
};

"use client";

import { MonitorIcon, MoonIcon, SunIcon } from "lucide-react";
import { useTheme } from "next-themes";

import { Button } from "@my-app/ui/components/button";
import {
  DropdownMenu,
  DropdownMenuContent,
  DropdownMenuRadioGroup,
  DropdownMenuRadioItem,
  DropdownMenuTrigger,
} from "@my-app/ui/components/dropdown-menu";

const themes = [
  { icon: SunIcon, label: "Light", value: "light" },
  { icon: MoonIcon, label: "Dark", value: "dark" },
  { icon: MonitorIcon, label: "System", value: "system" },
] as const;

export const ModeToggle = () => {
  const { theme, setTheme } = useTheme();

  return (
    <DropdownMenu>
      <DropdownMenuTrigger
        render={
          <Button aria-label="Toggle theme" size="icon" variant="ghost" />
        }
      >
        <SunIcon className="dark:hidden" />
        <MoonIcon className="hidden dark:block" />
      </DropdownMenuTrigger>
      <DropdownMenuContent align="end">
        <DropdownMenuRadioGroup onValueChange={setTheme} value={theme}>
          {themes.map(({ icon: Icon, label, value }) => (
            <DropdownMenuRadioItem key={value} value={value}>
              <Icon />
              {label}
            </DropdownMenuRadioItem>
          ))}
        </DropdownMenuRadioGroup>
      </DropdownMenuContent>
    </DropdownMenu>
  );
};

"use client";

import Link from "next/link";
import { usePathname } from "next/navigation";

import { cn } from "@my-app/ui/lib/utils";

const links = [{ href: "/", label: "Home" }] as const;

export const NavLinks = () => {
  const pathname = usePathname();

  return (
    <nav className="flex gap-1 text-sm">
      {links.map(({ href, label }) => (
        <Link
          aria-current={pathname === href ? "page" : undefined}
          className={cn(
            "hover:text-foreground rounded-md px-2.5 py-1.5 font-medium transition-colors",
            pathname === href
              ? "bg-muted text-foreground"
              : "text-muted-foreground"
          )}
          href={href}
          key={href}
        >
          {label}
        </Link>
      ))}
    </nav>
  );
};

  { href: "/", label: "Home" },
  { href: "/todos", label: "Todos" },
] as const;

export const NavLinks = () => {
  const pathname = usePathname();

  return (
    <nav className="flex gap-1 text-sm">
      {links.map(({ href, label }) => (
        <Link
          aria-current={pathname === href ? "page" : undefined}
          className={cn(
            "hover:text-foreground rounded-md px-2.5 py-1.5 font-medium transition-colors",
            pathname === href
              ? "bg-muted text-foreground"
              : "text-muted-foreground"
          )}
          href={href}
          key={href}
        >
          {label}
        </Link>
      ))}
    </nav>
  );
};

"use client";

import { QueryClientProvider } from "@tanstack/react-query";
import { ReactQueryDevtools } from "@tanstack/react-query-devtools";
import { ThemeProvider } from "next-themes";
import type { ReactNode } from "react";

import { Toaster } from "@my-app/ui/components/sonner";

import { getQueryClient } from "#src/lib/query-client.ts";

export const Providers = ({ children }: { children: ReactNode }) => {
  const queryClient = getQueryClient();

  return (
    <ThemeProvider
      attribute="class"
      defaultTheme="system"
      disableTransitionOnChange
    >
      <QueryClientProvider client={queryClient}>
        {children}
        <ReactQueryDevtools buttonPosition="bottom-right" />
      </QueryClientProvider>
      <Toaster richColors />
    </ThemeProvider>
  );
};

#   next-self-sqlite-docker-e2e
#   next-self-sqlite-expo-docker

"use client";

import { MotionConfig, motion, useReducedMotion } from "motion/react";
import { useEffect, useState } from "react";

import { cn } from "@my-app/ui/lib/utils";

import { DotGrid } from "#src/components/dot-grid.tsx";
import { StackPlate } from "#src/components/stack-plate.tsx";

interface Layer {
  readonly name: string;
  readonly role: string;
}

const assemble = {
  hidden: {},
  shown: { transition: { delayChildren: 0.1, staggerChildren: 0.08 } },
};

const drop = {
  hidden: { opacity: 0, y: -48 },
  shown: { opacity: 1, y: 0 },
};

const walkInterval = 550;

/** Calls `onStep` for each layer in turn, ending back on the first, and returns what stops it early. */
const walkLayers = (count: number, onStep: (index: number) => void) => {
  let step = 0;

  const timer = setInterval(() => {
    step += 1;
    onStep(step % count);

    if (step === count) {
      clearInterval(timer);
    }
  }, walkInterval);

  return () => {
    clearInterval(timer);
  };
};

/**
 * Each plate overlaps the one below it, and later elements paint on top, so the DOM runs bottom to top and
 * `flex-col-reverse` puts the first layer back on top.
 */
export const StackDiagram = ({ layers }: { layers: readonly Layer[] }) => {
  const reduced = useReducedMotion();
  const [hovered, setHovered] = useState(false);

  const [active, setActive] = useState(0);
  const walking = reduced !== true && !hovered;

  // A request travels down the stack once on load; pointing at a layer takes over from it.
  useEffect(
    () => (walking ? walkLayers(layers.length, setActive) : undefined),
    [layers.length, walking]
  );

  return (
    <MotionConfig
      reducedMotion="user"
      transition={{ bounce: 0.25, duration: 0.6, type: "spring" }}
    >
      <div className="relative isolate">
        <DotGrid />
        <motion.ul
          animate="shown"
          aria-label="The stack"
          className="flex flex-col-reverse pt-14 pb-14"
          initial="hidden"
          onPointerEnter={() => {
            setHovered(true);
          }}
          variants={assemble}
        >
          {layers
            .map((layer, index) => ({ index, layer }))
            .toReversed()
            .map(({ index, layer: { name, role } }) => (
              <li
                className="relative flex h-13 items-center pl-44 sm:pl-64"
                key={role}
                onPointerEnter={() => {
                  setActive(index);
                }}
              >
                <motion.div
                  className="absolute top-1/2 left-0 w-36 -translate-y-[44%] sm:w-52"
                  variants={drop}
                >
                  {/* The layers above the active one lift to reveal it. */}
                  <motion.div animate={{ y: index < active ? -20 : 0 }}>
                    <StackPlate active={index === active} />
                  </motion.div>
                </motion.div>
                <span
                  aria-hidden="true"
                  className={cn(
                    "absolute top-1/2 left-36 h-px w-6 transition-colors duration-300 sm:left-52 sm:w-10",
                    index === active ? "bg-primary" : "bg-border"
                  )}
                />
                <div className="flex flex-col">
                  <span className="text-muted-foreground text-xs">{role}</span>
                  <span
                    className={cn(
                      "font-medium transition-colors duration-300",
                      index !== active && "text-muted-foreground"
                    )}
                  >
                    {name}
                  </span>
                </div>
              </li>
            ))}
        </motion.ul>
      </div>
    </MotionConfig>
  );
};

import { cn } from "@my-app/ui/lib/utils";

/** One layer of the stack, drawn isometrically: the project's mark, from the home page to sign-in. */
export const StackPlate = ({ active }: { active: boolean }) => {
  const side = cn(
    "transition-colors duration-300",
    active
      ? "fill-primary/70 stroke-primary"
      : "fill-muted stroke-foreground/25"
  );

  return (
    <svg
      aria-hidden="true"
      className="size-full overflow-visible drop-shadow-lg"
      viewBox="0 0 200 114"
    >
      <polygon className={side} points="0,50 100,100 100,114 0,64" />
      <polygon className={side} points="100,100 200,50 200,64 100,114" />
      <polygon
        className={cn(
          "transition-colors duration-300",
          active
            ? "fill-primary stroke-primary"
            : "fill-card stroke-foreground/25"
        )}
        points="100,0 200,50 100,100 0,50"
      />
    </svg>
  );
};

"use client";

import {
  useMutation,
  useQueryClient,
  useSuspenseQuery,
} from "@tanstack/react-query";
import { ListTodoIcon, PlusIcon, Trash2Icon } from "lucide-react";
import { AnimatePresence, MotionConfig, motion } from "motion/react";
import { useState } from "react";
import type { SubmitEvent } from "react";

import { Badge } from "@my-app/ui/components/badge";
import { Button } from "@my-app/ui/components/button";
import { Card, CardContent } from "@my-app/ui/components/card";
import { Checkbox } from "@my-app/ui/components/checkbox";
import {
  Empty,
  EmptyDescription,
  EmptyHeader,
  EmptyMedia,
  EmptyTitle,
} from "@my-app/ui/components/empty";
import { Input } from "@my-app/ui/components/input";
import { Separator } from "@my-app/ui/components/separator";
import { Spinner } from "@my-app/ui/components/spinner";
import { cn } from "@my-app/ui/lib/utils";

import { api } from "#src/lib/api.ts";

export const TodoList = () => {
  const [draft, setDraft] = useState({ title: "" });
  const { title } = draft;
  const queryClient = useQueryClient();
  const { data: todos } = useSuspenseQuery(api.todos.list.queryOptions());

  const invalidateTodos = {
    onSuccess: async () => {
      await queryClient.invalidateQueries({ queryKey: api.todos.key() });
    },
  };

  const createTodo = useMutation(
    api.todos.create.mutationOptions(invalidateTodos)
  );

  const updateTodo = useMutation(
    api.todos.update.mutationOptions(invalidateTodos)
  );

  const deleteTodo = useMutation(
    api.todos.delete.mutationOptions(invalidateTodos)
  );

  const done = todos.filter((todo) => todo.completed).length;

  const handleSubmit = (event: SubmitEvent<HTMLFormElement>) => {
    event.preventDefault();
    createTodo.mutate(
      { title },
      {
        onSuccess: () => {
          // Keep whatever was typed while the request was in flight.
          setDraft((current) => (current === draft ? { title: "" } : current));
        },
      }
    );
  };

  return (
    <MotionConfig reducedMotion="user">
      <section className="mx-auto flex w-full max-w-2xl flex-col gap-8">
        <header className="flex flex-wrap items-end justify-between gap-4">
          <div className="flex flex-col gap-1.5">
            <h1 className="text-4xl font-semibold tracking-tight">Todos</h1>
            <p className="text-muted-foreground text-sm">
              Private to your account.
            </p>
          </div>
          {todos.length > 0 && (
            <Badge variant="secondary">
              {done} of {todos.length} done
            </Badge>
          )}
        </header>
        {todos.length > 0 && (
          <div className="bg-muted h-1 overflow-hidden rounded-full">
            <motion.div
              animate={{ scaleX: done / todos.length }}
              className="bg-primary h-full origin-left"
              initial={false}
              transition={{ bounce: 0, duration: 0.5, type: "spring" }}
            />
          </div>
        )}
        <Card>
          <CardContent>
            <form className="flex gap-2" onSubmit={handleSubmit}>
              <Input
                aria-label="New todo"
                maxLength={200}
                onChange={(event) => {
                  setDraft({ title: event.target.value });
                }}
                placeholder="Add a todo"
                value={title}
              />
              <Button
                disabled={createTodo.isPending || title.trim() === ""}
                type="submit"
              >
                {createTodo.isPending ? (
                  <Spinner data-icon="inline-start" />
                ) : (
                  <PlusIcon data-icon="inline-start" />
                )}
                Add
              </Button>
            </form>
          </CardContent>
          <Separator />
          <ul className="-my-4 divide-y empty:hidden">
            <AnimatePresence initial={false}>
              {todos.map((todo) => (
                <motion.li
                  animate={{ height: "auto", opacity: 1 }}
                  className="hover:bg-muted/50 flex items-center gap-3 overflow-hidden px-4 py-3 transition-colors"
                  exit={{ height: 0, opacity: 0, paddingBlock: 0 }}
                  initial={{ height: 0, opacity: 0 }}
                  layout="position"
                  transition={{ duration: 0.2 }}
                  key={todo.id}
                >
                  <Checkbox
                    aria-label={todo.title}
                    checked={todo.completed}
                    onCheckedChange={(completed) => {
                      updateTodo.mutate({ completed, id: todo.id });
                    }}
                  />
                  <span
                    className={cn(
                      "flex-1 text-sm wrap-anywhere",
                      todo.completed &&
                        "text-muted-foreground decoration-muted-foreground/50 line-through"
                    )}
                  >
                    {todo.title}
                  </span>
                  <Button
                    aria-label={`Delete ${todo.title}`}
                    onClick={() => {
                      deleteTodo.mutate({ id: todo.id });
                    }}
                    size="icon-sm"
                    variant="ghost"
                  >
                    <Trash2Icon className="text-muted-foreground" />
                  </Button>
                </motion.li>
              ))}
            </AnimatePresence>
          </ul>
          {todos.length === 0 && (
            <Empty>
              <EmptyHeader>
                <EmptyMedia variant="icon">
                  <ListTodoIcon />
                </EmptyMedia>
                <EmptyTitle>No todos yet.</EmptyTitle>
                <EmptyDescription>
                  Add one above. It is saved through the API to the database.
                </EmptyDescription>
              </EmptyHeader>
            </Empty>
          )}
        </Card>
      </section>
    </MotionConfig>
  );
};

"use client";

import {
  useMutation,
  useQueryClient,
  useSuspenseQuery,
} from "@tanstack/react-query";
import { ListTodoIcon, PlusIcon, Trash2Icon } from "lucide-react";
import { AnimatePresence, MotionConfig, motion } from "motion/react";
import { useState } from "react";
import type { SubmitEvent } from "react";

import { Badge } from "@my-app/ui/components/badge";
import { Button } from "@my-app/ui/components/button";
import { Card, CardContent } from "@my-app/ui/components/card";
import { Checkbox } from "@my-app/ui/components/checkbox";
import {
  Empty,
  EmptyDescription,
  EmptyHeader,
  EmptyMedia,
  EmptyTitle,
} from "@my-app/ui/components/empty";
import { Input } from "@my-app/ui/components/input";
import { Separator } from "@my-app/ui/components/separator";
import { Spinner } from "@my-app/ui/components/spinner";
import { cn } from "@my-app/ui/lib/utils";

import { api } from "#src/lib/api.ts";

export const TodoList = () => {
  const [draft, setDraft] = useState({ title: "" });
  const { title } = draft;
  const queryClient = useQueryClient();
  const { data: todos } = useSuspenseQuery(api.todos.list.queryOptions());

  const invalidateTodos = {
    onSuccess: async () => {
      await queryClient.invalidateQueries({ queryKey: api.todos.key() });
    },
  };

  const createTodo = useMutation(
    api.todos.create.mutationOptions(invalidateTodos)
  );

  const updateTodo = useMutation(
    api.todos.update.mutationOptions(invalidateTodos)
  );

  const deleteTodo = useMutation(
    api.todos.delete.mutationOptions(invalidateTodos)
  );

  const done = todos.filter((todo) => todo.completed).length;

  const handleSubmit = (event: SubmitEvent<HTMLFormElement>) => {
    event.preventDefault();
    createTodo.mutate(
      { title },
      {
        onSuccess: () => {
          // Keep whatever was typed while the request was in flight.
          setDraft((current) => (current === draft ? { title: "" } : current));
        },
      }
    );
  };

  return (
    <MotionConfig reducedMotion="user">
      <section className="mx-auto flex w-full max-w-2xl flex-col gap-8">
        <header className="flex flex-wrap items-end justify-between gap-4">
          <div className="flex flex-col gap-1.5">
            <h1 className="text-4xl font-semibold tracking-tight">Todos</h1>
            <p className="text-muted-foreground text-sm">
              Shared by everyone who opens the app.
            </p>
          </div>
          {todos.length > 0 && (
            <Badge variant="secondary">
              {done} of {todos.length} done
            </Badge>
          )}
        </header>
        {todos.length > 0 && (
          <div className="bg-muted h-1 overflow-hidden rounded-full">
            <motion.div
              animate={{ scaleX: done / todos.length }}
              className="bg-primary h-full origin-left"
              initial={false}
              transition={{ bounce: 0, duration: 0.5, type: "spring" }}
            />
          </div>
        )}
        <Card>
          <CardContent>
            <form className="flex gap-2" onSubmit={handleSubmit}>
              <Input
                aria-label="New todo"
                maxLength={200}
                onChange={(event) => {
                  setDraft({ title: event.target.value });
                }}
                placeholder="Add a todo"
                value={title}
              />
              <Button
                disabled={createTodo.isPending || title.trim() === ""}
                type="submit"
              >
                {createTodo.isPending ? (
                  <Spinner data-icon="inline-start" />
                ) : (
                  <PlusIcon data-icon="inline-start" />
                )}
                Add
              </Button>
            </form>
          </CardContent>
          <Separator />
          <ul className="-my-4 divide-y empty:hidden">
            <AnimatePresence initial={false}>
              {todos.map((todo) => (
                <motion.li
                  animate={{ height: "auto", opacity: 1 }}
                  className="hover:bg-muted/50 flex items-center gap-3 overflow-hidden px-4 py-3 transition-colors"
                  exit={{ height: 0, opacity: 0, paddingBlock: 0 }}
                  initial={{ height: 0, opacity: 0 }}
                  layout="position"
                  transition={{ duration: 0.2 }}
                  key={todo.id}
                >
                  <Checkbox
                    aria-label={todo.title}
                    checked={todo.completed}
                    onCheckedChange={(completed) => {
                      updateTodo.mutate({ completed, id: todo.id });
                    }}
                  />
                  <span
                    className={cn(
                      "flex-1 text-sm wrap-anywhere",
                      todo.completed &&
                        "text-muted-foreground decoration-muted-foreground/50 line-through"
                    )}
                  >
                    {todo.title}
                  </span>
                  <Button
                    aria-label={`Delete ${todo.title}`}
                    onClick={() => {
                      deleteTodo.mutate({ id: todo.id });
                    }}
                    size="icon-sm"
                    variant="ghost"
                  >
                    <Trash2Icon className="text-muted-foreground" />
                  </Button>
                </motion.li>
              ))}
            </AnimatePresence>
          </ul>
          {todos.length === 0 && (
            <Empty>
              <EmptyHeader>
                <EmptyMedia variant="icon">
                  <ListTodoIcon />
                </EmptyMedia>
                <EmptyTitle>No todos yet.</EmptyTitle>
                <EmptyDescription>
                  Add one above. It is saved through the API to the database.
                </EmptyDescription>
              </EmptyHeader>
            </Empty>
          )}
        </Card>
      </section>
    </MotionConfig>
  );
};

import { createORPCClient } from "@orpc/client";
import { RPCLink } from "@orpc/client/fetch";
import { createTanstackQueryUtils } from "@orpc/tanstack-query";

import type { AppRouterClient } from "@my-app/api";

const client: AppRouterClient = createORPCClient(
  new RPCLink({ url: () => `${window.location.origin}/rpc` })
);

export const api = createTanstackQueryUtils(client);

#   next-self-sqlite-docker
#   next-self-sqlite-docker-e2e
#   next-self-sqlite-expo-docker
#   next-self-sqlite-expo-docker-e2e
import {
  MutationCache,
  QueryCache,
  QueryClient,
  environmentManager,
} from "@tanstack/react-query";
import { toast } from "sonner";

const createQueryClient = () => {
  const queryClient: QueryClient = new QueryClient({
    defaultOptions: { queries: { staleTime: 60_000 } },
    mutationCache: new MutationCache({
      onError: (error) => {
        toast.error(error.message);
      },
    }),
    queryCache: new QueryCache({
      onError: (error) => {
        toast.error(error.message, {
          action: {
            label: "Retry",
            onClick: () => {
              void queryClient.invalidateQueries();
            },
          },
        });
      },
    }),
  });

  return queryClient;
};

let browserQueryClient: QueryClient | undefined;

export const getQueryClient = () => {
  if (environmentManager.isServer()) {
    return createQueryClient();
  }

  browserQueryClient ??= createQueryClient();

  return browserQueryClient;
};

import "server-only";
import { createAuth } from "@my-app/auth";
import { createDb } from "@my-app/db";

import { readEnv } from "#src/server/env.ts";

const createServices = () => {
  const env = readEnv();
  const db = createDb(env.DATABASE_URL);

  const auth = createAuth({
    baseURL: env.BETTER_AUTH_URL,
    db,
    secret: env.BETTER_AUTH_SECRET,
  });

  return { auth, db };
};

let services: ReturnType<typeof createServices> | undefined;

export const getServices = () => {
  services ??= createServices();

  return services;
};

# apps/web/src/server/orpc.ts · 4 stacks
#   next-self-orpc-docker
#   next-self-orpc-docker-e2e
#   next-self-orpc-expo-docker
#   next-self-orpc-expo-docker-e2e
import "server-only";
import { OpenAPIHandler } from "@orpc/openapi/fetch";
import { OpenAPIReferencePlugin } from "@orpc/openapi/plugins";
import { ORPCError, onError } from "@orpc/server";
import { RPCHandler } from "@orpc/server/fetch";
import { ZodToJsonSchemaConverter } from "@orpc/zod/zod4";

import { appRouter } from "@my-app/api";
import type { Context } from "@my-app/api";

const logServerError = (cause: unknown) => {
  if (!(cause instanceof ORPCError) || cause.status >= 500) {
    console.error(cause);
  }
};

const mount =
  (
    handler: RPCHandler<Context> | OpenAPIHandler<Context>,
    prefix: `/${string}`
  ) =>
  async (request: Request) => {
    const { response } = await handler.handle(request, {
      context: {},
      prefix,
    });

    return response ?? new Response("Not Found", { status: 404 });
  };

export const handleRpc = mount(
  new RPCHandler(appRouter, { interceptors: [onError(logServerError)] }),
  "/rpc"
);

export const handleOpenApi = mount(
  new OpenAPIHandler(appRouter, {
    interceptors: [onError(logServerError)],
    plugins: [
      new OpenAPIReferencePlugin({
        schemaConverters: [new ZodToJsonSchemaConverter()],
        specGenerateOptions: {
          info: {
            title: "my-app",
            version: "0.0.0",
          },
        },
      }),
    ],
  }),
  "/api"
);

import "server-only";
import { OpenAPIHandler } from "@orpc/openapi/fetch";
import { OpenAPIReferencePlugin } from "@orpc/openapi/plugins";
import { ORPCError, onError } from "@orpc/server";
import { RPCHandler } from "@orpc/server/fetch";
import { ZodToJsonSchemaConverter } from "@orpc/zod/zod4";

import { appRouter } from "@my-app/api";
import type { Context } from "@my-app/api";

import { getServices } from "#src/server/context.ts";

export const createContext = async (
  requestHeaders: Headers
): Promise<Context> => {
  const { db, auth } = getServices();

  return {
    db,
    session: await auth.api.getSession({ headers: requestHeaders }),
  };
};

const logServerError = (cause: unknown) => {
  if (!(cause instanceof ORPCError) || cause.status >= 500) {
    console.error(cause);
  }
};

const mount =
  (
    handler: RPCHandler<Context> | OpenAPIHandler<Context>,
    prefix: `/${string}`
  ) =>
  async (request: Request) => {
    const { response } = await handler.handle(request, {
      context: await createContext(request.headers),
      prefix,
    });

    return response ?? new Response("Not Found", { status: 404 });
  };

export const handleRpc = mount(
  new RPCHandler(appRouter, { interceptors: [onError(logServerError)] }),
  "/rpc"
);

export const handleOpenApi = mount(
  new OpenAPIHandler(appRouter, {
    interceptors: [onError(logServerError)],
    plugins: [
      new OpenAPIReferencePlugin({
        schemaConverters: [new ZodToJsonSchemaConverter()],
        specGenerateOptions: {
          info: {
            title: "my-app",
            version: "0.0.0",
          },
        },
      }),
    ],
  }),
  "/api"
);

import "server-only";
import { OpenAPIHandler } from "@orpc/openapi/fetch";
import { OpenAPIReferencePlugin } from "@orpc/openapi/plugins";
import { ORPCError, onError } from "@orpc/server";
import { RPCHandler } from "@orpc/server/fetch";
import { ZodToJsonSchemaConverter } from "@orpc/zod/zod4";

import { appRouter } from "@my-app/api";
import type { Context } from "@my-app/api";

import { getServices } from "#src/server/context.ts";

export const createContext = (): Context => ({ db: getServices().db });

const logServerError = (cause: unknown) => {
  if (!(cause instanceof ORPCError) || cause.status >= 500) {
    console.error(cause);
  }
};

const mount =
  (
    handler: RPCHandler<Context> | OpenAPIHandler<Context>,
    prefix: `/${string}`
  ) =>
  async (request: Request) => {
    const { response } = await handler.handle(request, {
      context: createContext(),
      prefix,
    });

    return response ?? new Response("Not Found", { status: 404 });
  };

export const handleRpc = mount(
  new RPCHandler(appRouter, { interceptors: [onError(logServerError)] }),
  "/rpc"
);

export const handleOpenApi = mount(
  new OpenAPIHandler(appRouter, {
    interceptors: [onError(logServerError)],
    plugins: [
      new OpenAPIReferencePlugin({
        schemaConverters: [new ZodToJsonSchemaConverter()],
        specGenerateOptions: {
          info: {

#   next-hono-sqlite-expo-docker
#   next-hono-sqlite-expo-docker-e2e
import "server-only";
import { proxy } from "hono/proxy";

import { readEnv } from "#src/server/env.ts";

export const proxyToServer = async (request: Request) => {
  const { pathname, search } = new URL(request.url);

  try {
    return await proxy(new URL(`${pathname}${search}`, readEnv().SERVER_URL), {
      raw: request,
      redirect: "manual",
    });
  } catch (error) {
    // The client went away, so no one reads the response; this is not a server error.
    if (request.signal.aborted) {
      return new Response(null, { status: 499 });
    }

    throw error;
  }
};

import "server-only";
import { createORPCClient } from "@orpc/client";
import { RPCLink } from "@orpc/client/fetch";
import { createTanstackQueryUtils } from "@orpc/tanstack-query";
import { QueryClient } from "@tanstack/react-query";
import { headers } from "next/headers";
import { cache } from "react";

import type { AppRouterClient } from "@my-app/api";

import { readEnv } from "#src/server/env.ts";

// Server Components call the API server directly, with the visitor's cookies.
const serverClient: AppRouterClient = createORPCClient(
  new RPCLink({
    headers: async () => {
      const requestHeaders = await headers();

      return { cookie: requestHeaders.get("cookie") ?? "" };
    },
    url: () => `${readEnv().SERVER_URL}/rpc`,
  })
);

export const serverApi = createTanstackQueryUtils(serverClient);

export const getQueryClient = cache(() => new QueryClient());

import "server-only";
import { createRouterClient } from "@orpc/server";
import { createTanstackQueryUtils } from "@orpc/tanstack-query";
import { QueryClient } from "@tanstack/react-query";
import { headers } from "next/headers";
import { cache } from "react";

import { appRouter } from "@my-app/api";
import type { AppRouterClient } from "@my-app/api";

import { createContext } from "#src/server/orpc.ts";

const serverClient: AppRouterClient = createRouterClient(appRouter, {
  context: async () => await createContext(await headers()),
});

export const serverApi = createTanstackQueryUtils(serverClient);

export const getQueryClient = cache(() => new QueryClient());

import "server-only";
import { createRouterClient } from "@orpc/server";
import { createTanstackQueryUtils } from "@orpc/tanstack-query";
import { QueryClient } from "@tanstack/react-query";
import { headers } from "next/headers";
import { cache } from "react";

import { appRouter } from "@my-app/api";
import type { AppRouterClient } from "@my-app/api";

import { createContext } from "#src/server/orpc.ts";

const serverClient: AppRouterClient = createRouterClient(appRouter, {
  context: async () => {
    await headers();

    return createContext();
  },
});

export const serverApi = createTanstackQueryUtils(serverClient);

export const getQueryClient = cache(() => new QueryClient());
